; ---- game code ----
; patches the game by the option bits at $0200
apply_options:
		lda #$00
		sta d686E
		lda #$00
		sta d686F
		lda basic_input_buffer
		cmp #$ff
		beq l0A16
		lda #$01
		sta d686F
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A16:
		lda #$01
		bit basic_input_buffer
		bne l0A22
		lda #$ad
		sta d47B5
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A22:
		lda #$02
		bit basic_input_buffer
		bne l0A2E
		lda #$bd
		sta l4C7C
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A2E:

		bit basic_input_buffer
		bne l0A3A
		lda #$bd
		sta l4C5F
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A3A:
		lda #$08
		bit basic_input_buffer
		bne l0A5D
		lda #$4c
		sta l43A3
		lda #$ae
		sta l43A3 + 1
		lda #$43
		sta d43A5
		lda #$ea
		sta d4BB3
		sta d4BB3 + 1
		lda #$60
		sta l4790
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A5D:

		inc d6870
; jumped to by: apply_options
